## 2026-10-19 DVB-T2 HEVC VideoToolbox fallback (#203)
Hardware start guard now begins on an HEVC IRAP NAL (CRA/IDR/BLA), not on the packet key flag, so RASL-led joins are held back. Give-up window is 3 s of input (was 4 s and 60 packets). Build and tests not run (owner on battery); verify on the Mac with the hw_guard test.
## 2026-10-19 Meteor LRPT capture (#199)
LRPT (std 61) runs a 2 Msps capture instead of 1 Msps when the radio allows it, so the 150 kHz channel stays inside the window while the pass tracker moves the centre (app/state/app_state.cpp applyBandwidth). Not built or run here; the centre-follow path (app/panels/passes_ui.cpp retuneTo) was already in place.
## 2026-10-19 Recording library
Library button in the diagnostics row (app/panels/recording_library_ui.cpp): lists the recordings folder with date, length, rate, centre, the mode they were recorded in (the sidecar description) and a mode to re-analyse in; Open makes one the IQ file source, Re-analyse also opens the chosen mode. listRecordingFiles() in core/src/source/iq_file.cpp reads only sidecars and file headers. New SigMF sidecars now carry the mode id as their description. Nothing deleted or moved. Not built or run on this machine.

## 2026-10-19 KAPP burst PHY and selective repeat
Branch kapp-028. New mode directory kapp (core/src/kapp, core/include/dect2/kapp, tests/kapp): burst builder (profiles 0-8, preamble of two Zadoff-Chu halves, 4x repeated header, LDPC blocks with CRC-16, pilots, RRC at 8 samples per symbol), streaming BurstReceiver (per-half preamble correlation, fractional timing, CFO from the halves then from the 942 known symbols, pilot tracking), ArqSender/ArqReceiver (each PHY block carries its message offset, so resends work at any profile), ProfileController, ACK burst, resampler. Written against the kris foundation API (kris_fec.h, kris_common.h); not built or run on the laptop, CI to check. Additions to the spec: fixed scrambler on coded bits, flag bit 3 marks an ACK burst, message-offset blocks instead of the 10-bit retransmit table.

## 2026-10-19 KAAL voice codec in C++
Branch kaal-codec-028. core/src/kaal/kaal_codec.cpp, core/include/dect2/kaal/kaal_codec.h: port of the 16 kbit/s MDCT codec (kaal_codec.py): sine-window MDCT (direct float64), 16 bands, 1.5 dB band energies, adaptive binary arithmetic coder, per-frame rate loop to exactly 320 bits, decoder sanity checks, concealment with the portable LCG noise, 320-sample delay. "kaal" added to DECT2_MODES. Tests in tests/kaal (golden bits and PCM from the Python, generator gen_golden_codec.py). Not built or run on this machine; CI will.

## 2026-10-19 KAAL streaming voice receiver in C++
Branch kaal-rx-028 (merged kaal-phy-028 and kaal-codec-028; the two golden headers are now kaal_golden.h (PHY) and kaal_golden_codec.h (codec)). core/src/kaal/kaal_rx.cpp, core/include/dect2/kaal/kaal_rx.h: dect2::kaal::VoiceRx, a chunked port of kaal_rx.py. The first 3 s gather the static parameters as in the Python (DC, FFT sweep correlation and per-period gain fit, matched filter, symbol-clock fit, preamble differential sync, CFO, fine slope); then the stream is replayed and run frame by frame: sweep removed per period with a small PLL on the period position, early-late check on the preamble for the symbol clock (not in the Python), per-frame gain fit and noise estimate mixed 50/50 with the running mean, max-log demap, deinterleave per 4-frame block (block offset from the syndrome score of the first 27 frames), LDPC, codec. KaalCodec::StreamDecoder added so the codec runs frame by frame with the same state as decode(). Tests: tests/kaal/test_kaal_rx_loopback.cpp (bit-exact clean, FER at 8 dB, +-200 Hz, mid-stream start) with a test transmitter in kaal_rx_gen.h. Not built or run on this Mac; CI will.

Tests tests/kaal/test_kaal_tx_*.cpp against goldens from gen_golden_tx.py. Written without a build on this machine; CI is the first compile and run.

## 2026-10-19 HFDL shared first-stage decimation and speed benchmark
Branch hfdl-fast-028 (from 0.2.8). HfdlReceiver::feed low-passes and decimates a wide capture once before the channels (F = floor(rate / (1.2 x (span + 8 kHz))), only for F >= 2, i.e. 64 MHz and up; 16 and 32 MHz unchanged). Output at 64 MHz is not bit-identical to before. New benchmark tests/hfdl/test_hfdl_speed.cpp (hfdl_speed): 2 s at 16/32/64 MHz, prints wall time per second of input, CI limit 1.0. Not built or run on this Mac; verified by reading only.

## 2026-10-19 HFDL shared FFT channeliser for 32 and 64 MHz captures
Branch hfdl-channeliser-028 (0.2.8 + hfdl-opt-028 + hfdl-fast-028). Above 16.5 MHz with at least 4 channels in band, HfdlReceiver runs one weighted-overlap-add filter bank (N bins of 32-64 kHz, 4N-tap Kaiser prototype, hop N/2, sub-band rate 2 rate/N) on the analysis thread and each channel takes the nearest bin plus a residual mixer at the sub-band rate; cost ~ input rate, not rate x channels. Up to 16 MHz the per-channel path is unchanged. Not run on this machine (CI): tests hfdl_speed (16/32/64 MHz, old vs new at 32 MHz) and hfdl_chan (3 bursts in a 32 MHz capture, same frame count as the old path).

## 2026-10-19 KAPP and KAFT as real modes (branch kapp-kaft-app-028)
KAPP (family 124, engine standard 126, telemetry 125) and KAFT (family 125, engine standard 127, telemetry 126) replace their WIP rows in the "Kris custom modes" group; KAVB stays WIP. Engine: KappLiveReceiver (kapp::BurstReceiver + a kapp::ArqReceiver per sender, a list of whole messages, repeats ignored, constellation of the last burst) and KaftLiveReceiver (kaft::FrameReceiver + kaft::Receiver session, hash-verified files saved to a user-chosen folder with a cleaned name, never overwritten, resume info, profile ladder with the profile the stepper would ask for). Both only listen: no ACK, ACCEPT or VERIFIED is sent (no half-duplex turnaround). RxTelemetry::kapp / kaft, poll slots 130 / 131, kNumFamilies 129, kPollSeqSlots 140. Panels app/kapp/kapp_ui.cpp, app/kaft/kaft_ui.cpp; TX options (profile, passes, KAPP text or file, KAFT file and width) and the IQ-file destination serve both. The TX is one-way and finite: the message or file is sent `repeat` times (KAFT repeats OPEN/START every 8 bursts so a receiver can join late); TxGenerator::finished() ends a session by itself and a file destination is written unpaced (txModeFinite). Fixes found on the way: kaft::FrameReceiver found only the first frame of a recording with digital silence between frames (rounding residue in the normalised autocorrelation; now an energy floor), KAPP bursts are limited to 1 s by the receiver (TX uses 1000 ms). Thresholds: P0 / P1 measured at FER 1e-2 (AWGN, 600 codewords, 0.25 dB): BPSK 1/4 -1.0 dB (n 2304) and -0.75 dB (n 7680), QPSK 1/4 +2.0 and +2.25 dB; the tables had -1.5 / -1.0 for P0 and -2.3 for P1 (spec estimate), now the measured values. Limits found: KAPP carrier offset capture is +-315 Hz (the two Zadoff-Chu halves are 1.59 ms apart), KAFT about +-320 Hz at 200 kHz and +-1.6 kHz at 1 MHz; KAFT receive costs about 1x real time of one core at 2.4 Msps in the test (not margin for a live radio), no compression codec, ladder not closed (no return link). Tests: kapp_engine, kaft_engine, kapp_kaft_mode.

## 2026-10-19 TETRA voice: traffic-channel decoding and per-call encryption check
Branch tetra-voice-final-028 (on top of the codec-loader wiring 2d8dfe15). Traffic channel decoder from the public ETSI EN 300 395-2 V1.3.1 clause 5.5: descrambling, the (24,18) matrix deinterleaver (the old a = 103 guess is gone), depuncturing (2/3 and 8/18), Viterbi of the 16-state rate 1/3 code, CRC-7 plus parity check, table 5 re-ordering into two 137 bit speech frames for the user-installed plug-in (core/src/tetra/tetra_traffic.cpp). Per-call encryption: the Rust MAC walk now reads call id, end-to-end encryption flag (D-SETUP, D-CONNECT, EN 300 392-2 clause 14) and the slot of the channel allocation; CallTracker (tetra_voice.cpp) lets a slot through only after a fresh set-up PDU said clear, on top of the cell gate. Encrypted or unknown: no frames. Stolen-frame half slots (5.6) and the AMR codec are not done. Tests: tests/tetra/test_tetra_traffic.cpp, Rust call_setup_encryption_flag. Not built or run on this Mac (the algorithm was checked with a Python port).

## 2026-10-19 eLoran / Loran-C receiver mode (WIP, Loran-C only)
Branch eloran-028 from 0.2.8. Family 126, engine standard 128, "eLoran / Loran-C (WIP)" in the Aviation group next to NDB and VOR, ModeTuning 90-110 kHz, 500 ksps (minimum 250 ksps), radio 30 kHz above the carrier. core/src/eloran/eloran_rx.cpp: matched filter on the complex baseband with the t^2 exp(-2t/65 us) pulse, comb over the 8 pulses at 1 ms, GRI scan over 40000..99990 us in 10 us steps (no chain list needed), fold at the GRI, peel the stations, then a coherent search over the four public phase patterns (master / secondary, A / B) that gives the code and the arrival time. eloran_engine.cpp mixes, decimates to 250 kHz or more, low-passes to +-11 kHz and analyses the last 2 s once a second; lock = same GRI twice with a master. eloran_gen.h makes synthetic chains for tests. Not done: the eLoran data channel (the Eurofix pattern table and code are not in the public text found), the master's 9th pulse phase, coding delays of real chains. At 0 dB (peak pulse power over noise in 20 kHz) the offsets scatter about 4 us rms over 2 s, which is the Cramer-Rao limit for 20 groups; within 2 us needs about 18 dB.

## 2026-10-19 KAVB physical layer and video container
Branch kavb-028. New mode directory kavb (core/src/kavb, core/include/dect2/kavb, tests/kavb); `kavb` appended to the single DECT2_MODES line, not yet wired into the app mode table (the integration step does that). PHY (kavb_phy, kavb_rx): profiles A to D (700 / 1050 / 1400 / 1400 ksym/s, 16-QAM, 64-QAM for D, LDPC n = 1920 rate 3/4) plus a robust QPSK rate 1/2 flag, 32-symbol Zadoff-Chu preamble, 64-bit header (profile, robust, FEC profile, sweep index, sequence, link quality, CRC-16) on a K = 7 rate 1/2 code, pilot every 16th symbol, pseudo-random bit interleaver across the 4 blocks of a frame, RRC 0.25 at 4 samples per symbol, V-shaped sweep at -10 dB (period = whole frames, about 0.5 s). Receiver: matched filter, split-preamble correlation, header decoded over a grid of residual frequencies (the sweep makes a closed-form estimate too noisy), maximum-likelihood pilot/preamble fit for frequency, phase and amplitude, sweep estimated per quarter frame and subtracted before a second matched-filter pass, max-log demapper, decision-directed 7-tap equaliser pass for multipath. Video/audio layer (kavb_av): packets (type, stream, seq, length, CRC-32), cells of one LDPC block, FEC groups with a GF(256) erasure code (new Rust module rust/onair_kris/src/erasure.rs, two new C ABI functions in rs/kris.h), fragmentation of codec units, pluggable PayloadSource / PayloadSink (no codec in the repo), synthetic AV source, bit budget per profile. Measured thresholds and what is not verified: see KAVB-MEASURED-v0.1.md.

core/src/kait, core/include/dect2/kait: own image codec (BT.601 4:2:0, CDF 9/7 lifting wavelet, embedded bitplane passes with neighbourhood contexts, binary range coder; any prefix of the stream decodes), framing (10 s burst, 158 ksym/s RRC 0.2 = 190 kHz, up/down sweeps at both ends for start, carrier and sample clock, a pilot per 31 symbols, 16x repeated K=7 rate-1/4 header with callsign/size/layer table, layers = n 7680 LDPC blocks with BPSK x8..x1, QPSK, 16QAM, 64QAM; 4 profiles), receiver (FFT sweep correlation, pilot tracking, prefix decode, picture per layer), KaitLiveReceiver for the engine (worker thread, telemetry with the picture), makeKaitTxGenerator. ## 2026-10-19 KAPP / KAFT carrier-offset capture and incremental KAFT front end (kapp-kaft-app-028)
KAPP: a second detector adds block powers (blocks of 8 preamble symbols, noise-only mean 1) and the offset is read from the phase between blocks, then removed before the old halves-based estimate; capture is now about +-5 kHz (tested at +-2.5 kHz with noise, P0 at 1 dB Es/N0 with +-2 kHz). KAFT: coarse offset from blocks of 16 symbols; the capture range is symRate x ZC root / 502 (a Zadoff-Chu root couples a symbol of timing with a frequency step): +-20 kHz at 1 MHz, +-8.7 kHz at 500 kHz, +-2.2 kHz at 200 kHz (root 7); tested at +-5 kHz (1 MHz) and +-2.1 kHz (200 kHz) with noise. kaft::FrameReceiver is incremental (per-width resampled stream and matched-filter output kept, only new samples converted, symmetric matched filter): 10 s of 2.4 Msps in about 3 s of CPU (0.3 x real time, was about 1x); test asserts under 5 s x ciSlack. Not done: a CRC-32 / message id for KAPP messages (an app-layer trailer would not interoperate with other KAPP senders).
## 2026-10-19 KAAL codec v3, 160-symbol real-time frames, station ID in the sweep
Branch kaal-v3-028 (from 0.2.8). Codec v3 (core/src/kaal/kaal_codec3.cpp, kaal_codec3_tables.h): port of kaal_codec_v3.py, profiles 12/16/24/32 kbit/s (240/320/480/640 bits per 20 ms frame), 26 bands, 4.5 dB energies, trained start probabilities per profile, the profile in the first two bits of every frame; bit stream and decoded PCM identical to the Python on the golden vectors (tests/kaal/gen_golden_codec3.py, gen_codec3_tables.py). PHY: the 202-symbol frame (25.25 ms per 20 ms of speech, TX 26 % slower than real time) is replaced by a 160-symbol frame (32 preamble + 120 data + 8 pilots = 20 ms) carrying a rate-matched word of 480 bits (the (640,320) LDPC shortened to k = 240 or 320 information bits, first 480 - k parity bits sent, ldpcEncodeRm/ldpcDecodeRm): 12 kbit/s is rate 1/2, 16 kbit/s rate 2/3, both exactly real time; 24 and 32 kbit/s take two words per codec frame, i.e. twice the air time (a file/recording choice). The receiver tries k = 240 and 320 and reads the profile from the decoded frame header. Sweep: period 24240 samples with four variants (up, down, fast1, fast2) = 2 bits per period carrying a 6-character base-40 callsign + 4-bit suffix class + CRC-8 (12.12 s cycle), core/src/kaal/kaal_sweepid.cpp; receiver SweepProc (per-period variant decision, exact analytic-template removal, per-variant position trackers) and SweepIdScanner (sweep-only ID reader on a worker thread, used by KaalLiveReceiver while the voice receiver is not locked). TX: VoiceTx::setBitrate/setSweepCallsign, TxRequest opt "kaal.bitrate" (default 16), TX panel combo, telemetry bitrateKbps/sweepId. VoiceTx(0) keeps the legacy v0.1 stream for the golden TX test, the receiver no longer decodes it. Notes: scratchpad/KAAL-V3-NOTES.md. Built and run with clang++ on the kaal sources only (tests kaal_codec3, kaal_rx_loopback, kaal_sweepid, kaal_tx_v3, kaal_engine and the old kaal tests); the full CMake/Rust build and the GUI were not run.
## 2026-10-18 Satellite beacon
New mode satbeacon (family 107, active 108, std 109, worktree dvbs-028). Built for the owner's capture onair_general_11705.250MHz_2Msps (a Ku-band beacon): carrier at 11705.486 MHz drifting with the LNB, a second CW carrier 545.64 kHz above, the main carrier phase modulated by a 48 kHz subcarrier with 4800 bit/s bi-phase data in 2048-bit frames on the IRIG 106 sync word FAF320 with a counter 0-31 in byte 4 (the format DecSAT describes for Intelsat 19's beacon; recognised from its README, no code taken). First attempt read the stream at byte level (600 baud, 256 "bits"): the frames repeated but were meaningless; the bi-phase chips at 9600/s gave clean frames (all 457 of the 224 s recording, no errors). Lessons: look at the tone phase flips AND the chip-level waveform before choosing a bit rate; the SDR clock is a few ppm off, so a bit clock needs a tracking loop, not a fixed rate. Tests: generator golden frames, chain, impairment sweep, live, engine.

## 2026-10-18 TV sat beacon decoder: decoded view
Renamed from Satellite beacon and moved to the TV group. The frames were analysed word by word over all 512 frames of the recording: sync FAF320, byte 3 FE, counter byte 4, last byte = sum8 of the others (512/512), byte 15 a 1.000 Hz seconds counter, byte 122 a copy of the counter, constants (00 00 1E A3 2B), FB fill, ~20 commutated words, ~60 samples; no ASCII, date, orbit or transponder table. Meanings are the maker's, so the Decoded view shows raw, grouped words with a confidence; the satellite is estimated (BSAT-4b 110E, 214 kHz off) from DecSAT's table of published Ku beacons (data ported, notice kept). DecSAT's status file also says the word meanings are open.

## 2026-10-20 Synthetic signals and TX checks for the KA modes (kris-synth-028)
Branch kris-synth-028 (integ-final3-028 + kavb-app2-028 + kaws-app-028 + integ-final2-028; KAWS telemetry slot moved from 133 to 136 because KAVB uses 133). KAPP (engine std 126), KAFT (127), KAIT (133), KAVB (125) and KAWS (135) each got a registered "Synthetic" source signal like KAAL: core/src/<mode>/<mode>_synth.cpp (header <mode>_gen.h), registered in makeModeSynth (core/src/engine/modes.cpp). All five are the real TX generator played as a loop by TxLoopSynth (core/include/dect2/engine/synth_loop.h: new generator when one finishes, carrier offset, noise from the measured signal power). modeOpt[0]: 0 = default profile, n = profile n-1. KAPP: three texts in turn, +1000 Hz. KAFT: demo text file, fixed session, 200 kHz width, +1500 Hz. KAIT: built-in picture, 16 s gap (new TX option kait.gap, default 2 s unchanged: a receiver decoding on a busy machine missed the next sweep with 2 s), +500 Hz. KAVB: profile A test stream, +3000 Hz. KAWS: chat then short beacon in the slots of the computer clock (generator waits for the next even slot, receiver follows the same clock; decodes within about one to two slot lengths), dial at -20 kHz tuning offset +30 Hz. Tests: test_<mode>_synth (through the engine) and test_<mode>_live_fuzz (noise, silence, constant, full-scale random, sparse spikes; blocks 1/7/96/4801/65536) for each; no receiver crashed or locked on noise. TX end to end (TxSession to an IQ file decoded by the receiver) already existed in test_kapp_engine, test_kaft_engine, test_kavb_engine, test_kaws_engine, test_kait_iqfile.

## 2026-10-21 JT4 checked on a real recording
WSJT-X sample DF2ZC_070926_040700.WAV (JT4A, 1.1 MB, kept outside the repo) decodes as "K1JT DF2ZC JO30", same as jt9. It sends the sync bit inverted and starts 5.1 s into the minute: the receiver now searches both sync polarities and starts up to 5.5 s late; candidate de-duplication no longer drops the tone-pair alias 2 bins away. Regression test with the same shape on synthetic audio.

## 2026-10-21 Analogue TV on weak signals (analog-tv-029)
Line sync for weak signals: when the threshold slicer finds no line structure, the acquisition folds the signal at every possible line period (matched filter for the sync pulse, period scan, significance against the neighbouring periods, tip-highest and depth checks, 625/525 before 405/819) and the line loop then runs on a block template (32 lines added at the loop's own phase, sync pulse and both edges found in it, loop corrected from that) instead of per-line threshold crossings; broad field-sync pulses by their mean over 22 us. Clean signals (sync height over 1.8 times the noise) go back to the slicer. Picture: temporal noise reduction (AtvReceiver::setNoiseReduction, automatic by the video S/N, motion adaptive). Measured with the generator (PAL B/G, NTSC M, SECAM D/K, 10 Msps): the line lock held down to C/N 12 dB before (NTSC 8), now to 3 dB; mean luminance difference to the card at C/N 12 went from 72 to 32; tests/atv/test_atv_weak.cpp holds the numbers. Not verified on a real off-air weak signal. The systems of issue #73 were already all present (A, C, E, H, K', L, L', NBTV); MUSE is not implemented.

## 2026-10-21 DAB transmitter list/map (#249)
Cause of the empty list: the TII detector needed frames without TII as its noise reference, so networks that send TII in every null symbol (most) found nothing; and dab-tii/ in OnAir-channels holds no data yet (no sites for any country). Fix: reference-free detection from the same null symbol (tested), Map tab keeps every transmitter heard over all channels (band scan waits for TII), table shows ids/ensemble/channel/level even without a site, markers clickable (tune), fullscreen = map full height with table at the side, "export heard" CSV (same columns as dab-transmitters.csv). Own file may be TSV; rows without a position are ignored. Unverified on real German signals. No freely licensed German site dataset found (FMLIST DABLIST licence unclear; Ofcom OGL covers UK only, download blocked to scripts): positions come from FIG 0/22, the user's file, or the crowd list.

## 2026-10-22 DTMB stutter issues (#88, #184, #254), branch fix-dtmb-030
No receiver fault found. Checked: the 554 MHz recording (PN945 16QAM 0.8 mode 2) decodes 38142/0 codewords, TS clean (no CC errors, PCR = 19.2513 Mbit/s net rate, radio MP2 PTS track arrival within 9 ms), same with 4096-sample chunks; dtmbtool sweep at 30 dB over PN420/595/945 x mode 1/2 x 4/16/32/64QAM/4QAM-NR x 0.4/0.8 + C=1 all clean. Findings: the radio services in that mux carry no PCR at all (PCR PID 0xa12.. never sent; live player does not need it), TV services are AVS+ (FFmpeg cavs refuses it). ## 2026-10-22 DAB tuning, scan and transmitter list (fix-dab-030: #262, #249, #210)
#262 cause: 0.2.9 (cb21daf8f) made the spectrum axis follow the radio centre during sticky tuning, but the engine mixed the decode shift into the samples before the spectrum, so after a click inside a wide capture every signal slid by the shift (marker on the wrong one, the next click jumped to the other side). The shift is now mixed just before the first receiver (spectrum, signal id, second mode and monitor see the raw capture; tests/engine/test_decode_shift.cpp). Also: a new frequency from the tuner (DAB channel list, typed) kept the shift, so the receiver decoded beside the channel (tunedrag::tunerRetune; gain changes keep it); the DAB band scan, scan list, Map tab and ensemble follow kept a click shift (dabTuneMhz clears it); Stop scan put the old frequency in the box without retuning. Bandwidth detection is skipped while a shift is active. #249: own-list parsing moved to app/dab/dab_txlist.h (tested), "import list..." merges a CSV (an export filled in, a shared regional list) into dab-transmitters.csv, a row with only lat or lon is no longer placed at 0, format in docs/dab-transmitters.md. ## 2026-10-22 DAB audio continuity audit (dab-audit-030)
Synthetic audit (tests/dab/test_dab_audit.cpp, 24 conditions + service switching, a sound-card model measuring silence and cutouts per minute; docs/DAB-AUDIT.md has the tables). Found and fixed: the sample clock error was not followed inside a frame (at 4.5 dB and +-100 ppm no audio at all, now none lost); the FFT window followed the strongest path (SFN 0 dB echo in the guard: 5.2 s silence/min, now 0); soft bits normalised per frame (fades poisoned the de-interleaver); a fade ended the lock after 4 frames and re-estimated the CFO from noise (now held up to 1.15 s); lost USB blocks cost 4 frames and a full search (now a quick re-acquisition, the missed CIFs go in as erasures aligned by the FIG 0/0 CIF count); no impulse blanker (impulsive noise: FIB 74 % -> 100 %); one uncorrectable RS code word dropped the whole superframe; lost audio was just missing (now concealment with crossfades, gap filling, 0.2 % rate control against the sound card's clock, 400 ms start level). New dab_playout.{h,cpp} + test_dab_playout. UI: "Audio x % concealed" with an RS/AU/sync tooltip, "Clock ppm"; rx JSON carries the counts. Modes II-IV not implemented (withdrawn from the standard). Not verified on a real radio.

## 2026-10-22 DRM audit: smooth audio (drm-audit-030)
Synthetic harness tests/drm/test_drm_smooth.cpp (45-case survey, simulated sound device, dropouts and made-up seconds per minute; 24 regression cases with thresholds, label slow) and tests/drm/test_drm_playout.cpp. Found: the tracker gave up after 160 ms without guard correlation (every fade over 0.2 s cost 4-5 s, 17-35 restarts per 90 s with holes), loops and clock estimate ran on noise in fades and followed the multipath centroid (-584/+670 ppm on channels 4/6), Doppler estimate read 0 Hz at 2 Hz, audio came in 1.2 s bursts with a 250 ms device start (start-up underrun), lost frames were silence (xHE-AAC: missing samples), no clock drift compensation. Fixed: hold through fades with background re-synchronisation, Doppler from pilot correlations at gy/2gy, 5-tap Wiener time interpolation below 1 Hz (about 1 dB: 16-QAM 9 dB 4.3 -> 1.2 % frames lost), per-multiplex-frame decoding, fade gates and FAC confirmation, clock offset from consistent windows, new drm::Playout (queue, pitch-repeat concealment with crossfades, latency kept, sinc resampler following the device: -300..+300 ppm without underrun). Hole 0.3 s / 15 s: 8.8 -> 0 s/min of dropouts; 5 s holes: 34 -> 17 s/min, no restarts. Numbers in docs/DRM-AUDIT.md. Not verified on a real signal; drm_engine and the app not run locally (CI).

## 2026-10-22 Spectrum and waterfall revamp (spectrum-revamp-030)
#270 cause: the 0.2.9 FFT commit moved the spectrum's frequency labels outside, which gave only the spectrum a 12 px right margin, so it was narrower than the waterfall under it. Flush plots now drop that overhang (specaxis::rightOverhang). Also: the waterfall picture starts half a bin left (bins drawn around their centre), the history moves along on a retune, the plot under the mouse draws first (no one-frame lag between the two), and a drag on the waterfall past the band retunes like one on the spectrum.
#246 (desktop part): a shown, unfocused window with a live waterfall keeps 30 fps; spectrum frames the UI misses are repeated as rows so the time scale stays true. The Android loop lives in the other repo. Also fixed: the saved FFT size never reached the engine at start.
#243: FFT 128k/256k (cap SpectrumAnalyzer::kMaxFft); waterfall Detail 16k/32k/64k columns (cap 64k, never more than the FFT bins; the view texture stays 8192).
#274: the FM channel filter edges can be dragged on the spectrum and the waterfall (the general receiver had it). Fixed-filter modes unchanged.
#264: impulse noise blanker (dect2::NoiseBlanker, in Engine::ingest after cleanSamples, before the spectrum and decoders), off by default, threshold 2-40x the mean, width 2 us-2 ms, blanked % shown.
Colours: new maps Magma, SDR++ classic, Ice, Fire, Night red, High contrast, Custom gradient (presets, stops); CB tags; per-mode remembered map; Brightness/Contrast/Gamma at the top of the Waterfall menu; trace colour presets (dark/light); hold-line colour, min hold, live line under the average; marker layer (right click: add/remove/clear). Pure headers app/gfx/spec_axis.h and colormap.h; tests spec_axis and noise_blanker. Not built locally (syntax checks + the two logic tests only): CI is the test bed.

Metal takes the drawable at draw time. App Nap held off only while a receiver runs. ## 2026-10-22 Whole-span detector (wide-detector-030)
Carrier finder: per-cell noise floor (20th percentile of +-12 cells, bounded by the span's, smoothed 0.5 s), hysteresis, minimum SNR + dwell, a signal over two adjacent cells is one carrier (no 10 dB dip), drifting carriers keep their id across a cell boundary, unmodulated lines rejected (new cells only), width measured, known channels (frequency database + band plan bands that list the mode) at a 3 dB lower threshold, optional priors-only. Bank: 0.5 s pre-roll replay for every mode (at most 2 M samples), the mixer follows drift, carriers survive a retune inside the span (CarrierFinder::shift), CPU budget (80 % of real time: "too slow", the weakest dropped every 2 s, never the listened one; back by one per 5 s), per-carrier IQ WAV recording. New wide modes: MPT1327, Yaesu Fusion, LTR, EDACS (ACARS/VDL2/HFDL/airband/TETRA/AIS are already multi-channel). Table: status line, width/age/held, sort, filter, Listen/Tune/Rec, settings popup (saved). Test: tests/engine/test_wide_detector.cpp (8/8 detected, 0 false; hump floor 0 false vs 8 with one floor; about 3 s).

## 2026-10-22: signal identifier revamp (branch identifier-030)
New features in sig_ident: spectral shape (Welch tones, tone spacing, spectral comb), MFSK tone spacing from the discriminator histogram, roll-off, a constellation guess (matched filter, u^M coherence, amplitude levels), OFDM carrier spacing and guard fraction; LoRa chirp test averaged per stretch (data no longer cancels the tone); low roll-off PSK and PSK31 no longer fall into pulses / GMSK; FSK second-harmonic rate caught by the equal-pairs test; AM carrier tolerance 4 % of the width. Signature table now three layers: hand-written, measured on each mode's generator (sig_known_gen.cpp, test_specx_idmatrix --signatures, 24 rows), and rows from the mode list for the rest (usual frequency only, confidence capped at 20 %). Verify step on the best three (sync, clock at the exact rate, cyclic prefix, chirp width, bursts, deviation) with per-candidate verdicts. Ties of look-alike modes are "ambiguous", not "unknown". Card: per-candidate evidence and verify tags, Open on every candidate, Why? table from featureRows(), Details per row of Identify all. New test specx_ideval (30 synthetic cases, impaired, confusion matrix, ~2 s): top-3 87 % at 15 dB, 93 % at 25 dB. idmatrix --quick 39 -> 52 % top-1 (measured rows; hand-written modes unchanged), moved to the slow tier; idfeatures out of the timing tier (ciSlack). docs/IDENTIFIER.md. Not verified: real off-air signals; built against a prebuilt 0.2.9 library locally, CI is the real build.

## 2026-10-22: Voice decryption with user-supplied keys (engine + P25/NXDN)
New core/src/voicekeys/ subsystem: standard cipher primitives (RC4, DES per FIPS 46-3, OFB, 15-bit scrambler; AES reused from meshcrypto), a key store (model, CRUD, CSV import/export, per-call matching, legal-accepted gate), per-call Decryptor + lookupForCall, and encrypted-at-rest persistence (AES-256-CTR + HMAC-SHA256, master key from an OS-keychain provider hook or a PBKDF2 passphrase fallback). Nothing is decrypted by default; no key search, recovery or cracking; restricted algorithms (TETRA TEA1-4) never implemented. P25 (TIA-102.AAAD ADP / DES-OFB / AES-256-OFB with MI, keyId and algId from HDU/LDU2) and NXDN (scrambler / DES / AES) receivers apply a matching user key before the vocoder and show per-call status text; with no matching key the call is reported and skipped exactly as before. DMR vendor Basic/Enhanced Privacy framing is proprietary and unpublished, so it is documented and left at detect-and-skip (not reverse-engineered); dPMR cipher-field parsing not yet added. Test tests/voicekeys/test_voice_keys.cpp (RC4/DES known-answer, synthetic-frame round-trip, wrong-key flagging, nothing decrypted without a key or before the legal notice, CSV, passphrase and master-key persistence) passes standalone. docs/KEYS.md added, docs/CODECS.md updated. Not done here: the native Keys GUI window + first-use legal dialog, and the macOS/Windows/Linux keychain providers (core exposes setSecretProvider; the passphrase fallback works and is tested).

The P25 and NXDN call panels show the core's status text with an "Add key..." button; DMR and dPMR get a "Voice keys..." link. Keychain providers in app/platform: macOS Keychain (SecItem), Windows Credential Manager, Linux libsecret by dlopen (passphrase fallback when absent). Key files (.oavk, voicekeys/ folder, onair-voice-keys*) are refused by the zip writer (core key_guard.h); a test also scans the web server and remote API sources for any reference to the key store. parseKeyHex now rejects odd digit counts. Tests: voice_keys_win. Not run here: the GUI and the three keychains (no full build, no screen).

## 2026-10-22 Scheduler and recordings manager (scheduler-030)
Tools > Scheduler (app/panels/scheduler_ui.cpp, core/src/sched/sched.cpp): jobs at a time / every N min / daily / weekly / cron-like (local zone, DST: a skipped hour runs just after the gap, a repeated hour runs once) / satellite pass above N deg (existing TLE data via passesFindSat + findPasses, lead time); action = tune freq, mode, radio, gain, start the receiver, record audio / IQ / receiver log, then stop and restore the previous radio state. Overlaps: higher priority cuts a running job, equal waits (queue, max wait, min run) or is skipped; missed jobs (sleep, closed): skip + note, or run late within a grace time; wake/clock-back warning; disk guard stops and warns below a free-space limit (default 2 GB); file-name template ({date} {time} {mode} {freq} {job} {sat} {n:3} ...); scheduler.json + scheduler_history.json in the data folder; the tick is in floatingWindows (like pass tracking). Tools > Recordings (app/panels/recmgr_ui.cpp, core/src/sched/recmgr.cpp): all recording folders in one table (search words / tag: kind: mode: fav >100MB <5min, sort), WAV playback, picture view, IQ open as file source, exact-sample trim (SigMF, raw IQ, WAV), SigMF sidecar for raw IQ, cs8/cu8/ci16/cf32 conversion, rename, trash with undo (per-folder .trash, 14 days), tags/notes/favourite in recindex.json, per-folder size budget + max age, auto cleanup every 30 min that never touches favourites or files written in the last 2 minutes. ## 2026-10-23 Ham contacts on the map (ham-map-feed-030)
Server > Share ham contacts on the map (off by default, own consent text, needs the map master switch and its consent). Decoded FT8/FT4/WSPR/JS8 contacts and logged QSOs go out as "ham" tracks with the hints m, f, b, to, tlat, tlon in extra (ingest API unchanged). Locators are rounded to the 4-char square centre (6 only if chosen); no notes, RST, QRZ data or exact location. ## 2026-10-22 NFC / RFID 13.56 MHz receiver (mode-nfc-030)
New mode (family 146, engine standard 148, active standard 147, telemetry slot 141, id "nfc"). Receive-only decoder of ISO/IEC 14443 Type A (REQA/WUPA, ATQA, anticollision + SELECT CL1-3, SAK, UID sizes, BCC, CRC_A, HLTA, RATS/ATS, APDU hex, NDEF text/URI from Type 2 READ), Type B (REQB/ATQB, CRC_B) and ISO/IEC 15693 (1-of-4/1-of-256 reader PPM, inventory, 64-bit UID, system info). DSP: carrier mixed to 0 Hz, field envelope for 100% ASK Miller reader frames, 847.5 kHz (14443) / 423.75 kHz (15693) subcarrier down-mix + 3-pole LP for Manchester tag answers; a burst segmenter splits reader vs tag by whether the field drops. Protocol layer core/src/nfc/nfc_proto.cpp, demod nfc_rx.cpp, engine wrapper nfc_engine.cpp, synthetic generator nfc_gen.cpp, panel app/nfc/nfc_ui.cpp (frame log, tag table, NDEF). Needs any HF receiver at >=2.4 Msps so the +-847.5 kHz sidebands fit. Phase 2 active reader (core/src/nfc/nfc_tx.cpp, makeNfcReaderTxGenerator): continuous 13.56 MHz carrier with 100% ASK Miller REQA/anticoll/SELECT/READ/GET_VERSION, through the existing TX gate only, with a 13.553-13.567 MHz ISM band gate (nfcTxFrequencyAllowed) and a low amplitude cap; dry-run in tests, nothing keyed. docs/NFC.md (physics, loop/isolation antenna guide, limits, FDX-B 134.2 kHz pet-chip note as a documented LF extra, not folded in). Tests: tests/nfc/test_nfc_decode.cpp (CRCs, Miller codec, NDEF, loopback clean + 20 dB SNR + 12 kHz offset, 40 noise-only runs = 0 false frames; all pass standalone), tests/app/test_nfc_mode.cpp (wiring). Clean-room from public ISO/IEC 14443-2/-3/-4 and 15693 summaries and the NFC Forum Type 2/NDEF docs; no encrypted content decoded, no cloning, no key attacks. Not verified on real hardware: the demodulator on real pulse shapes, real (weak) load modulation and real noise is untested; thresholds/bandwidths are engineering choices from the public timing rules. Built and run standalone (nfc_proto/nfc_rx/nfc_gen + test); the full engine/app integration is left to GitHub CI.

## 2026-10-22 SBAS and ASTERIX modes (branch mode-sbas-radar-030)
Two new modes. SBAS (family 150, active 151, std 152, poll slot 150): the GNSS receiver restricted to the SBAS PRNs 120..158 with no GPS lock needed (replanGps: when the mask holds SBAS/QZSS but not GPS, gpsAcq searches their PRNs and auxAcq stays empty; startFromHit uses gpsAcq then), a new setSbasCallback on GnssReceiver (the 250 message bits of every message with a good CRC), and core/src/sbas/sbas_msg.cpp, a content decoder for types 0-7, 9, 10, 12, 17, 18, 24, 25, 26 (PRN mask, fast corrections with UDRE, integrity, degradation, GEO navigation, UTC offset, almanac, IGP mask and delays, long-term corrections) kept per satellite. Layouts from the public DO-229 description, cross-checked against RTKLIB's sbas.c (reference only). Test signal: the GNSS generator's three SBAS satellites with structured content (modeOpt[3] bit 2, off by default so the GNSS tests are unchanged). Screens: satellites, messages, fast corrections, ionosphere, navigation. Tests: sbas_msg, sbas_engine (slow: clean, +-5 kHz with 20 ppm, -20 ppm, 38 dB-Hz), sbas_mode. QZSS L1 C/A was already in GNSS; L1S (SLAS) and L6 NOT done: no code or message description available to build them clean-room.
ASTERIX (family 151, active 152, std 153, slot 151 reserved): no radio. core/src/asterix: asterix_proto (data blocks, FSPEC, items of CAT 021 ed 2.6 UAP, 048 ed 1.21, 062 ed 1.18, track fields; unknown item length ends the record), asterix_engine (AsterixReceiver in the Engine object with its own threads: UDP unicast/multicast on loopback unless LAN is ticked, raw file or classic pcap reader, demo traffic, track table, positions from range/azimuth and Cartesian with a user-set origin marked approximate). Tests: asterix_proto (hand-built records, round trips, fuzz), asterix_engine (loopback UDP, pcap both byte orders, aging, demo), asterix_mode; sanitizer runs clean on the laptop. Not tested on a live radar. Weather radar: nothing added (a composite image viewer was ruled out).
Open: numbers 150/151 are new, check for a clash with other branches (kNumFamilies 160, kPollSeqSlots 160); translations not added (the i18n test only warns).

## 2026-10-22: Argos and Sigfox modes (mode-argos-sigfox-030)
Two new mode entries, receive only. Argos (family 148, active 149, engine std 150, slot 143; issue #256): PTT uplink 401.650 MHz with the SARSAT-style burst finder, PTT-A2 Manchester +-1.1 rad demodulator (timing and rate searched, then refined over the whole message), 28 bit ID + user data, per-platform burst frequency list with drift; A3/ZE/Argos-4 bursts only listed (CNES specs not public). Downlink 465.9875 MHz: squared-signal acquisition, 60 Hz squaring loop through the pass Doppler, Manchester bits, HDLC deframing NRZ / inverted / NRZI with the X.25 FCS, measured Doppler against passesArgosDoppler() (MetOp-B/-C added to the pass planner). Sigfox (family 149, active 150, engine std 151, slot 144): wrapper on the ISM SigfoxRx, whose frame layer now follows the public Sigfox radio specification (sigfox_proto: frame types, Table 3-1, CRC init 0 checked against the renard README example frame, rank 2/3 convolution codes, zones RC1-RC6), both D-BPSK phase conventions tried, a per-symbol drift correction. Tests: tests/argos (proto, rx loopback with noise/offsets/drift/Doppler pass), tests/sigfox (proto, rx loopback), tests/app/test_argos_sigfox_mode.cpp; tests/ism/test_ism_sigfox.cpp still passes. Docs: docs/argos.md, docs/sigfox.md. Not verified on real signals: everything (no Argos or Sigfox IQ was available).

## 2026-10-22 ROS (identify only) (mode-ros-030)
ROS (EA5HVK) is proprietary. No public source describes its framing, FEC, interleaver, alphabet or spreading, so it cannot be decoded. docs/ROS.md lists what is known (sigidwiki, radioscanner.ru, IK1AMC, amateurradio.com) and what is missing. What was built: core/src/ros/ros_detect.cpp, which recognises one tone at a time on a 12-20 Hz grid about 2.2 kHz wide and names the sub-mode (4, 8 or 16 Bd) from the tone-change timing. It is used by the Signal Identifier through a "ROS" row that is proposed only when the grid is found and opens in General USB. The v7.4.0 frequencies are in hf.tsv. tests/ros/test_ros_detect.cpp uses random tones on the reported grid, which is not ROS encoding. Not verified on a real ROS recording.

## 2026-10-22 Marine VHF voice (vhf-marine-030)
New mode Maritime > Marine VHF (family 148, active standard 149, engine standard 150; 146/147 were taken by KLW/TDCS). Channel plan as data/bandplans/marine_vhf_channels.csv (compiled in like the airports; regions INT, EUR, BAL, USA, CAN; A/B designators; ITU rule checked by tests), core/src/marinevhf: channels (lookup, up/down, DSC text parser), ChannelScan (whole-span and single-tuner, 16 priority) and CallLog, MarineVhfReceiver = WideBank of narrow-FM channel receivers (quieting squelch, 30 ms look-ahead, call log, WAV per transmission, play choice). wide_bank.h got a bind hook (absolute frequency of a new carrier) and a cheaper pre-roll history. DSC screen: Listen on VHF channel N button. Tests: tests/marinevhf (3 programs) pass standalone; app, engine files syntax-checked only, GitHub CI is the build test. Docs: docs/marine-vhf.md.

## 2026-10-22 Long-range and satellite AIS (sat-ais-030)
Branch sat-ais-030. AIS now decodes up to four channels at once in two pairs placed by the radio's centre: AIS 1 + 2 and the long-range 75 + 76 (156.775 / 156.825 MHz); one 6-8 Msps capture at 159.4 MHz holds all four, a narrow radio hops (60 s / 200 s, ais::aisTunePlan). Message 27 per ITU-R M.1371-5 table 79 (encode/decode, position accuracy, RAIM, GNSS latency flag); table "LR", dashed ring on the map, per-message receive time stamps (AisRxRecord, AisTelemetry::recent). Satellite mode (Li, Chen, Chen, Acta Astronautica 2017, doi:10.1016/j.actaastro.2017.02.014): wide Doppler filters, per-channel Doppler tracking, collision decoder (power-step windows, frequency candidates incl. TLE-predicted Doppler of known ships, successive cancellation), AisSatStats (passes, new-type ratio, type mix, SNR vs elevation, monthly detection probability, CSV) and the AIS tab's Satellite view with pass prediction and arming. Tests: tests/ais/test_ais_longrange.cpp, test_ais_satellite.cpp (all existing AIS tests that link standalone pass). Limits: equal-power collisions within ~4 dB are lost; nothing verified on a real satellite recording; pass arming not run live. docs/SATELLITE_AIS.md.

## 2026-10-22: ISDB-S (BS satellite TV), branch mode-isdbs-030
New mode "ISDB-S (BS)": **family 154, engine standard 156 (active standard 155), poll slot 154**, kPollSeqSlots 155, kNumFamilies 160; nothing else of 154-159 used. New directories core/src/isdbs, core/include/dect2/isdbs, app/isdbs, tests/isdbs; `isdbs` appended to the single DECT2_MODES line. Receiver (isdbs_rx.cpp): DvbsFront for matched filter and timing, coarse carrier from the 8th power (plus 0 Hz, plus a grid of +-14 x 0.0048 symbol rate every fourth failed window), frame sync by a 32 symbol BPSK word two frames apart, carrier from the 192 known 4-symbol bursts (smoothed line fit, alias of 1/207 settled by the two halves of the sync word), TMCC (CRC-16 slices, 8 per super-frame, decoding starts at frame 0 of a super-frame), per-slot decoders (isdbs_slot.cpp: Viterbi K=7 with DVB-S puncturing for BPSK/QPSK, own 8-state Viterbi for TC8PSK, byte sync, ConvInterleaver, RS(204,188), dvbt descrambler), packets of a stream's slots merged by stream bit position. Generator (isdbs_gen.cpp): same chain forwards with RRC shaping, CFO, clock offset, noise; synth for the app's built-in source (isdbs_synth.cpp, demo TV programme in a 16-slot TC8PSK stream plus QPSK 3/4 and BPSK streams). Engine wired like DVB-C (packet callback to onTsPackets, so the player, services and recorder work), app screen app/isdbs/isdbs_ui.cpp, mode row in group 0. Tests: tests/isdbs/test_isdbs.cpp (ctest `isdbs`, all pass locally standalone, 6 s), tests/app/test_isdbs_mode.cpp (text checks). Thresholds measured on generated signals: TC8PSK 2/3 from 10 dB Es/N0, QPSK 1/2 about 4 dB, 3/4 6 dB, 7/8 9 dB. Lessons: packets of the slots of one stream must be merged by stream position, not by the order the decoders happen to emit them (they decode in batches); bursts 207 symbols apart alias every 1/207 cycles per symbol, so a frequency estimate from the 32 symbol sync word is needed to pick the right one; a sync metric above threshold on one occurrence was not enough, require both. **Not verified: any real BS signal.** The frame constants are from memory of public descriptions; the sync words, TMCC layout, 8PSK trellis, burst PN and packet-to-slot mapping are our own choices (listed in docs/ISDBS.md), so a real transponder is not expected to decode. Engine and app pieces compile-checked (-fsyntax-only), not linked or run; the CI build and test_isdbs / test_isdbs_mode are the first full check. No radio in the app can sample 39 Msps unless it is a Pluto / bladeRF / Lime / USRP class device.

## 2026-10-22 P25 Phase 2 and EDACS voice following (mode-p25-edacs-030)
New mode P25 Phase 2 (family 180, engine standard 182, active 181; kNumFamilies 181, kPollSeqSlots 182): TDMA 6000 sym/s differential QPSK, 8 parallel sample-phase demodulators + 40-bit sync search (also measures CFO and SNR), slot clock over a 12-slot superframe, ISCH/DUID, 4V/2V voice bursts, SACCH/FACCH MAC PDUs (RS(46,26)/RS(52,30) over p25::rs + CRC16), MAC_PTT/END_PTT/ACTIVE/IDLE/SIGNAL and the TSBK-equivalent messages (grants, update, UU grant, RFSS/net/adj status, IDEN_UP(_VU)) into a call table (per slot), talkgroup table, site and adjacent sites. Encrypted: from the PTT algorithm ID only, ESS areas are never read; voice of an encrypted (or not yet known) call never reaches the tap or the codec. Voice = AMBE+2 through NxdnAmbe (plug-in "ambe2" or mbelib), per-slot decoder, "Listen to" first heard / slot 1 / slot 2. Files core/{include,src}/p25p2, app/p25p2, tests/p25p2 (proto, rx loopback: clean, SNR 20..6 dB, CFO -400..+350 Hz, rates 192/240/300/480 kHz, lead-in alignments; plugin: stub ambe2 plug-in, slot choice, encrypted). NOT verified: every field position and the ISCH/I-ISCH code, the burst layout, interleave, CRC polynomial and AMBE+2 bit order are written from memory of public descriptions without the standards at hand (all in p25p2_proto.h); H-CPM downlink and the real pulse shape; any real signal. Decode floor in the loopback test: ~12 dB SNR clean, ~9 dB with losses, none at 6 dB.
EDACS (family 130) extended: edacs_msg (message layer with a ONE-TABLE layout that is this program's ASSUMPTION, not a public fact), call table, talkgroup table, site info, call log, frequency table of LCNs (user text, saved in the data dir), FFT power scan of the voice channels with a debounced carrier detector, follow of one analog FM voice channel (announced calls, optional carrier-only follow, group filter, outside-capture notes). No ProVoice codec, nothing encrypted. Tests: edacs_msg, edacs_follow (generated whole system with control channel + three voice channels, noise, CFO +-1.5 kHz). Mode numbers used: 180 (P25 Phase 2) only; 181/182 are its active/engine standard. Not verified: any real EDACS system (message layout, LCN numbering of a real site, ProVoice).

## 2026-10-22 Signal Lab (signal-lab-030)
Tools > Signal Lab: blind analysis of unknown digital signals from the spectrum selection (Identify / Shift+drag keeps its samples while the window is open, sigid::Capture::keepSamples), a recording, pasted bits or a test signal. Six pure stages in core/src/signallab (dect2_signallab): channel/bursts, modulation family with confidences (envelope, x^n lines, inst-freq k-means, cyclic prefix), symbol rate by four methods + Gardner/PLL recovery, line codes + LFSR descramblers, sync/frame/checksum search (27-entry catalogue), fields + bit viewer, exports incl. the Mode Builder JSON (docs/SIGNAL-LAB.md). Evaluation harness (test_signallab evaluate N seed; ctest signallab_eval, slow): 80 signals: rate 96 %, family 90 %, frames bit-exact 60 %, checksum 79 %.

## 2026-10-22 Mode Builder (mode-builder-030)
Tools > Mode Builder: a no-code decoder. The JSON mode definition (docs/MODE-BUILDER.md, version 1, shared with the Signal Lab) gives modulation (OOK/ASK, FSK 2/4/8, GFSK/GMSK, PSK 2/4/8, DPSK), pulse shape, line code (NRZ, NRZI, Manchester, Thomas, differential Manchester, Miller, 4b5b, 8b10b), polarity, bit order, preamble, sync word with bit errors, additive / multiplicative scrambler, frame length (fixed, length field, end flag with HDLC stuffing), FEC (repetition, Hamming incl. extended, convolutional K 3-9 r 1/2 1/3 Viterbi, Reed-Solomon any n,k,m,poly,fcr,prim), block interleaver, CRC (20 presets or custom, SUM8/XOR8, range, position, endianness), fields (uint/int/bool/enum/bitflags/ascii/bcd/float32/lat/lon/hex, scale, offset, units) and display templates. Core library dect2_modebuilder (core/src/modebuilder): streaming receiver (mixer, decimating FIR, discriminator/envelope/carrier loop, Gardner clock with burst acquisition by Oerder-Meyr timing, level fit and M-th power frequency estimate, squelch), frame engine trying every polarity / PSK rotation / line-code phase, generator for round trips, CSV/JSON export, six examples in data/modes compiled in. Window: Form and JSON views with validation messages, Examples / Saved / Open, Save to <data>/modes, Test on recording, Generate and decode / save test signal, frame table, export. Register as mode: a row in the new CUSTOM MODES group (families from 20000, registered.txt), live on receiver 1 through the plugin sample path; the permanent "Mode Builder" row (19999) lists them. Tests: tests/modebuilder (CRC vectors incl. CCITT/IBM/CRC-32, all line codes, scramblers, interleaver, every FEC, broken JSON and every validation message, round trips of all examples and 13 modulations / 20 framing variants with noise, CFO, clock error, off-centre at 2x rate, streaming in small pieces, noise-only false-frame check).

## 2026-10-23: DTMB TV sound in DRA through the add-on (dtmb-dra-030)
Issue #266 comment (DRA works for CDR but not DTMB audio). New core/src/tv/tv_dra.{h,cpp}: TvDraDecoder cuts PES payloads into DRA frames (0x7FFF sync, size in words, frames may span PES), feeds the user-installed add-on through cdr::draApi() (same loader as CDR, no new loader), resamples to 48 kHz and stamps the PCM with the PES times. Player: DRA streams (snapshot codec "DRA") that FFmpeg lists as data/unknown become extra audio tracks after the FFmpeg ones (codec "DRA", language from the PMT/ISO 639 descriptor), packets from av_read_frame go through emitAudio (the old pushAudio tail, now shared) so gap fill, clock and A/V sync are unchanged; add-on installed while playing is picked up. PMT: stream_type 0x14 -> DRA audio (rust/onair_ts si.rs; from memory of GY/T 220.1, unverified; "DRA1" registration already worked). UI: TV notice "DRA audio: install the DRA add-on to hear this service" (or "cannot be used (reason)") with ? opening the DRA help (DraTv help removed, merged into Dra), DRA row in Help > Voice codecs. Test: tests/tv/test_tv_dra.cpp (synthetic TS, stream parser, FFmpeg demux, fake add-on tone, split frames, resample, no add-on). UNVERIFIED against real DTMB DRA audio or the real decoder.

## 2026-10-22 Propagation tool rework (prop-rework-030)
Branch prop-rework-030. Model fixes: VHF/UHF had a ~47 dB step at the radio horizon (free space, then P.526 far field) and no ground
reflection; now P.526 3.2 smooth earth with its LOS blend + plane earth + P.452 troposcatter, P.2108 clutter, rain, gases, polarisation.
HF absorption used the D-layer-reflected ray angle (43 dB at 7.1 MHz / 1000 km, now ~19 dB); ground wave got curvature and polarisation,
above-MUF loss, K index, P.533 excess loss, atmospheric noise, Friis receive chain. New: prop_scenario (presets, SDR noise figures, checks,
verdict, budget, sweeps, CSV), prop_terrain (SRTM .hgt / GeoTIFF, delta-Bullington), antenna pattern file hook. Screen redesigned: Simple /
Advanced toggle, compare A/B, link-budget table with tooltips, sweeps incl. HF MUF/LUF day chart. Tests: tests/prop/test_prop_{model,scenario,terrain}.cpp.

## 2026-10-22 Antenna designer (antenna-030)
Tools > Antenna designer (app/antd, core/src/antd, docs/ANTENNA.md): clean-room thin-wire MoM (mixed-potential EFIE, Galerkin triangles, reduced kernel, closed-form static part), junctions, ground half-bases, delta-gap sources (complex, several, or driving a line end), R+jX / RLC loads, skin-effect wire loss, TL two-ports in parallel at a gap (crossed, VF, loss), free / perfect / real ground (quasi-static images + Fresnel far field), blocked threaded complex LU (n=1500: 0.8 s on 1 thread, 0.26 s on 8). Outputs: Z/SWR sweep, 3D pattern + az/el cuts, dBi/dBd, F/B, F/S, beamwidths, take-off, efficiency, directivity, currents, near field (core), convergence check. 19 templates with handbook formulas + trim to resonance (magloop C auto-tuned), Nelder-Mead optimiser, calculators (cable table, mismatch loss, L/stub/quarter-wave/gamma/balun, line transform, EIRP, AF, band presets), NEC deck import/export, pattern CSV for Propagation (dataDir()/antennas, readPatternCsv/patternGainDbi). Tests test_antd_mom (dipole 0.5 lambda 77.5+j42.4, resonant 0.488 lambda 71.9 ohm, 2.16 dBi; monopole exactly half, 5.17 dBi; short dipole 20 pi^2 (L/lambda)^2; loop resonant 1.06 lambda 126 ohm; 2-el Yagi 6.4 dBi F/B 10.6; energy balance; refinement; TL lambda/2 and lambda/4) and test_antd_tools. Not verified: the window itself (syntax-checked only, no local app build), real-ground impedance against NEC Sommerfeld, the slim jim and discone templates (rough impedances).

## 2026-10-23: KAAL voice codec 4 (KV4)
Branch kaal-codec-030. New own speech codec for the four KAAL v4 voice slots, separate module core/src/kaalvoice (interface core/include/dect2/kaalvoice/kaal_voice.h, docs/KAAL-CODEC.md); no PHY/framing file touched. 8 kHz analysis-by-synthesis core (KLT-predictive LSF quantiser, fractional pitch, pyramid-VQ pulse codebook, joint gains, postfilter) + parametric 3.6-8 kHz high band with time envelope; profiles 3.2/4.8/6.0/8.0 (the V32R/V48/V60/V80 budgets of docs/KAAL-SLOTS.md: 40 ms payloads, in-band speech/SID bit) and HQ12/HQ16; VAD/DTX with 40-bit SID and comfort noise, concealment, importance classes A/B/C, optional input noise suppressor. Old KaalCodec3 / KaalCodec v0.1 behind the same FrameCodec interface, bit-exact. Objective scores only (no listening): KV4 8 kbit/s about equals old 12 kbit/s on low-band SNR with lower loudness disturbance; HQ16 beats old 16k on the synthetic set, old 16k keeps the better BLD on the real narrow-band sample. Deterministic (own math, -ffp-contract=off): same golden digest on arm64 and x86-64. RTF about 0.01 per stream on the Mac; Pi 4 not measured. Tests: kaalvoice_roundtrip, _robust, _dtx, _golden; tool kaalvoice_bench. Not done: wiring into the KAAL v4 TX/RX (PHY agent), a listening test, a Pi 4 measurement.
## 2026-10-23 KAAL frame version 4 (kaal-phy-030)
KAAL v4 puts four TDMA voice slots in the 20 kHz channel: 16 ksym/s, a 40 ms superframe with a 1 ms up + 1 ms down chirp sync, a QPSK control channel and 4 x 144-symbol slots. Slot budgets for the codec are 8.0 / 6.0 / 4.8 / 3.2 kbit/s (docs/KAAL-SLOTS.md; kaal_slots.h with registerSlotCodec).
Receiver: chirp-pair acquisition (timing and carrier in one step, +-4 kHz), pilot/sync tracking, an MMSE equaliser from the chirps, DD phase smoothing, iterative re-estimation from the LDPC soft output, and layered LDPC. v3 words now use the layered decoder too: 1 % FER 0.5 / 0.85 dB lower.
Measured: V60 at 1 % PER needs 8.0 dB Es/N0; mean lock time 0.12 s; text goes from 10 B/s (v3 beacon slice) to 3168 B/s with all slots free, and 64 B/s with all four voice slots busy.
Wiring: TX generator kaal.frame=4 (TX and IQ file); the live receiver runs v4 beside v3; new panel options. Tests: test_kaal_v4, test_kaal_v4_impair (long), test_kaal_v4_live.
Not done: v4 voice codec (other agent), over-the-air test, slot interleaving for fast fading, multi-transmitter TDMA. Details in docs/KAAL-NEXT.md.
## 2026-10-23: ISDB-S rebuilt on ITU-R BO.1408-1, branch isdbs-spec2-030
Replaced the guessed ISDB-S framing of 2026-10-22 with the one in ITU-R BO.1408-1. The owner allowed one download: the free English PDF, 606 KB, kept in the scratchpad. ARIB TR-B15 and STD-B21 were used as well.
* **Header:** W1 1B95, 64 TMCC bits and W2 A340 / W3 5CBF, through the rate 1/2 code, BPSK.
* **TMCC:** 384 bits with RS(64,48), 8 blocks a super-frame, its own PRBS. It is applied two super-frames late (TR-B15).
* **Coding:** one K=7 code over the whole frame, puncturing restarted per group. TC8PSK = coded B0 + uncoded B1, with the Fig. 15 maps.
* **Slots:** one 203 byte packet each, groups most efficient first, dummy slots not sent.
* **Dispersal and interleaving:** PRBS 1+x14+x15 per super-frame after RS; byte interleaving across the 8 frames.
* **Bursts:** squared, never relied on.
* **Code and tests:** new core/src/isdbs/isdbs_fec.{h,cpp} replaces isdbs_slot.*. Generator and tests rewritten: Table 3/4 numbers, RS(64,48), a frame coding loop, and the receiver end to end, including random bursts and a TMCC change with no lost packet.

Lesson: at low Es/N0 a symbol timing slip inside a frame costs a whole super-frame, because of the interleaving. Narrowing the timing loop after lock (0.01 -> 0.002) fixed the BPSK / QPSK 1/2 RS failures at 3 dB.

Thresholds on generated signals: TC8PSK 9 dB, QPSK 1/2 3 dB, BPSK 1/2 1 dB.

Still guessed (docs/ISDBS.md lists them): details of the TMCC field order, where dummy slots sit inside a group, PRBS clocking over skipped bytes, the burst PN.

**No real signal tested.** Only the standalone test_isdbs was built and run. The app/engine build was not run and CI was not dispatched.

## 2026-10-23 TETRA voice echo + single carrier (tetra-echo-030)
Issue #267 (audio repeats at 1.8 Msps). No sample recording was attached to any TETRA issue (#267/#213/#241/#274 hold only crash logs), so the cause was not reproduced on air; hardened where duplicates or dropouts could arise. (1) decodeCarrier drops a frame of the followed slot whose frame number is at or just behind the one expected (voiceDup): repeated slots in a capture, overlapping or doubly decoded captures. (2) Carrier names across captures are matched within 9 kHz (was a 25 kHz grid cell: a carrier near a cell edge changed name each capture, losing call state, timeline and the voice stream). (3) Each candidate carrier's frames are buffered (FrameBuffer) and only the carrier with speech reaches the sink, so silence from one carrier and speech from another are not both played in one capture. (4) Carriers: Search span / Single carrier (TetraReceiver::setSingleCarrier, pref tetraSingle): one 25 kHz channel at the dial frequency, no spectrum search. Test: tests/tetra/test_tetra_echo.cpp (fingerprinted bursts: consecutive, doubly decoded and overlapping captures give each burst once).
## 2026-10-23 TETRAPOL voice and polarity (tetrapol-voice-030, #241 #273)
The old TETRAPOL frame coding was an OnAir test format; it is now PAS 0001-2 clause 6 (voice and data frames: circular rate-1/2 code with a tail-biting Viterbi, VHF/UHF interleaving, UHF precoding, 127-bit scrambling, CRC-3/CRC-5), with PAS 0001-3 data blocks (single, dual, multi-frame with parity repair), HDLC FCS, TPDU UI/DT and the TSDUs D_SYSTEM_INFO, D_GROUP_ACTIVATION, D_CALL_START, D_CALL_CONNECT and the call ends. tetrapol-kit (GPL-2) was read as a cross-check of the tables only (credits row, open licence point for the owner). Receiver: streaming discriminator with an early-late clock, sync on three frame headers, polarity found automatically (both tried, kept by CRC; Normal/Inverted override, #273), band coding and scramble found by CRC statistics, frames before the lock replayed. Voice: user-installed plug-in onair-codec-tetrapol (16-byte frame: 120 RP-CELP bits MSB first + damaged flag, 160 samples of 8 kHz back), only for calls whose own signalling carries key index 0; encrypted and unknown calls stay silent and are counted. Listen selector, per-call WAV (date/time file names only), call and group-call tables on screen only (never saved). Tests: test_tetrapol_rx (tables, round trips at every scramble, polarity/band/offset/clock/SNR matrix, manual override, noise) and test_tetrapol_voice (gate, plug-in layout, fake codec, a real tone plug-in through the loader into a WAV). Verified on synthetic signals only: the issues held no sample recordings, so nothing was checked on air.
## 2026-10-23 Receive beamforming (beamform-030)
Direction finding > Beamformer: delay-and-sum, MVDR (diagonal loading) and LCMV null steering for a chosen azimuth/elevation (3D via Array::z), up to 4 beams (BeamBank), auto-steer to the strongest MUSIC peak, polar beam pattern, noise gain, beamwidth and null depth. BeamIqSource exposes one beam as an IQ source for the receiver chain. Core in core/src/df/beam.cpp, test tests/df/test_df_beam.cpp (simulated 5-element UCA, element gain errors, noise-source calibration). Not hardware-tested; the Beam 1..4 entries are not yet in the device list.
## 2026-10-23 Synthetic wideband (synth-wideband-030)
core/include/dect2/dsp/synth_wide.h + core/src/dsp/synth_wide.cpp: 2-4 narrow radios (same nominal rate, adjacent overlapping slices) stitched into one wide complex stream (per-slice sqrt-Hann FFT, raised-cosine cross-fade over the overlaps, M-IFFT WOLA). Alignment from the overlap: coarse frequency offset (power-spectrum cross-correlation), fine offset (cross-spectrum phase per hop), delay + phase (peak of the transformed cross-spectrum; integer part shifts the ring), gain (power ratio), clock drift (slope of delay over time fed back into a cubic FracResampler). Status: overlap mismatch dB, offset Hz, drift ppm, locked, dropouts; a stalled or removed radio is left out (weights rebuilt) and re-aligned on return. Output (outRate, centerOffsetHz) feeds the wide-bank/channelizer like any wide capture. Test tests/dsp/test_synth_wide.cpp (synthetic 3-radio scenario with injected gain/phase/delay/ppm/offset, dropout and return), registered as synth_wide. NOT done: radio-picker Group UI and the engine source wrapper (core only). Coherence is not possible across free-running dongles for phase-sensitive modes.

## 2026-10-23 Vulkan GPU LDPC for Linux (gpu-vulkan-030, issue #39)
core/src/dsp/gpu_ldpc_vulkan.cpp replaces the Linux stub (Android keeps it): the Metal DVB-T2/S2 and DTMB kernels ported to GLSL (core/src/dsp/vulkan/*.comp), SPIR-V committed in ldpc_spirv.h (tools/gen_vulkan_spirv.sh, glslangValidator + spirv-val, run once in the Linux container), libvulkan opened at run time, hand-declared API subset (vulkan/vk_min.h, layouts checked against vulkan_core.h). Picks discrete > integrated > virtual, skips CPU implementations; self-test at start (T2 decode + DTMB against a CPU copy; a DTMB failure only moves DTMB to the CPU); device loss/timeout -> CPU for good. Env: DECT2_NOGPU or DECT2_VULKAN=0 force CPU, DECT2_VULKAN=1 also accepts lavapipe, DECT2_GPU_ADAPTER=<index>. New test gpu_ldpc_exact (any backend vs a scalar copy of the schedule, to the bit, clean/threshold/failing blocks): passes on lavapipe and on Metal (M2 Pro). dtmb_gpu passes on lavapipe (1710 words, 0 mismatches; channel decoder gives the same TS). lavapipe is slower than the CPU (DTMB 3.7-4.3 s vs 1.5-3.0 s on one thread): says nothing about real GPUs. Not tested: any real Vulkan GPU (NVIDIA/AMD/Intel), Windows (D3D11 unchanged), MoltenVK.

Notice + ? help (CodecHelp::AvsPlus), Voice codecs row, CODECS.md section. Test: tests/tv/test_avsplus.cpp (stock mpeg1video borrowed as the stand-in). Not verified: any real AVS+ stream, the real add-on, its decoder name, pixel format.

New own speech codec for the four KAAL v4 voice slots, separate module core/src/kaalvoice (interface core/include/dect2/kaalvoice/kaal_voice.h, docs/KAAL-CODEC.md); no PHY/framing file touched. 8 kHz analysis-by-synthesis core (KLT-predictive LSF quantiser, fractional pitch, pyramid-VQ pulse codebook, joint gains, postfilter) + parametric 3.6-8 kHz high band with time envelope; profiles 3.2/4.8/6.0/8.0 (the V32R/V48/V60/V80 budgets of docs/KAAL-SLOTS.md: 40 ms payloads, in-band speech/SID bit) and HQ12/HQ16; VAD/DTX with 40-bit SID and comfort noise, concealment, importance classes A/B/C, optional input noise suppressor. Cause: the waterfall history was drawn against the current radio centre, so after a click-retune old rows sat at the wrong frequency. Fixed by the spectrum-revamp-030 alignment (Waterfall::follow shifts rows on retune); added: follow also while paused, and a retune alignment test (tone column vs new axis, with/without tune offset, band jump clears) in tests/app/test_spec_axis.cpp.
## 2026-10-23 3D waterfall (spectrum3d-030)
Waterfall popup > 3D view: the history as a height field (as SDRangel / SDRoxide), drawn through ImDrawList (no new GPU code, both Metal and GL): screen-space mesh in painter's order (strips and columns from both ends towards the eye, so no depth buffer), coloured with the waterfall's palette and levels plus light slope shading, peak-hold ridge on the back edge, tuned-channel plane and its width on the floor, frequency and time labels. Mouse: drag orbits, Shift/right/middle drag pans, wheel zooms, Ctrl+wheel height, double click back to the angle, click on the surface tunes (ray march + bisection pick, through clickTuneMhz so each mode's raster/limits apply). Keys over the plot: arrows (Shift: pan), +/-, 1-5 angles (Angled/Front/Top/Side/Low), [ ], Space freeze, Home. Touch: pinch zoom/pan, one finger orbits (w3d::applyGesture fed from touch::gesture()). Rows 64-1024: own decimated ring fed only with new rows (keeps more than the main 512). Works on any mode's spectrum and on wide and narrow spans: the columns are those in the spectrum's frequency view. Budget: a column per 3 device px, at most 768 x 160k vertices (Low power: 5 px, 320 cols, 40k vertices, rebuild at most 15 Hz, no shading; picked up through __has_include of dect2/power/power.h once perf-power-030 is merged). The mesh is rebuilt only on a new row, a camera move or a resize; other frames copy the cached vertices.
Pure parts app/gfx/wf3d.h (+ wf3d_draw.h); test tests/app/test_wf3d.cpp (projection/ray inverse, presets, decimation keeps a 1-bin carrier, budget, ring == rebuild, painter's order, 16-bit chunks, a software-rasterised picture of the real draw list checked by pixel, pick on floor/wall/top/smoothed). Measured (M-series, -O2, 16384 bins x 512 rows, 1600 x 700 px plot, 533 x 256 grid, 140k vertices / 817k indices): frame with a new row 2.1 ms CPU (mesh 1.8), frame without 0.15 ms, about 7% of one core at 30 rows/s and 60 fps; Low power 0.28 ms; pick 0.01 ms. GPU fill cost of ~270k triangles not measured. A one-bin spike is a sliver that thins to its top (no wider than one column). Dev flag --wf3d <angle> for screenshots. Palettes and noise blanker: spectrum-revamp-030 (db2c4ca87) cherry-picked as the base.
Status correction (2026-10-23): the --wf3d screenshot check did not complete and the full app build did not complete either, so the 3D waterfall has not been seen on screen and the app target with it has not been shown to build. Only the pure parts (app/gfx/wf3d.h) and their test were checked.

## 2026-10-23 Docking for the new interface (#242, branch docking-030)
No Dear ImGui docking branch on this machine (only master 1.92.2b and 1.87 copies), nothing downloaded: the app docks its own windows. View > Docking > Use docking (Ctrl+Alt+D; Cmd+Option on macOS, where ImGui swaps Cmd and Ctrl; off by default, not with the touch interface): the body becomes a dock space; the built-in panels (Modes, Spectrum, List, Analysis, Log) are windows, the tool windows (Map, Memory, Passes ...) keep drawing themselves and the existing window placement hook (the touch one; never on together) puts each docked window into its box. Drag a title onto the drop targets (a cross per box, four at the dock-space edges) to dock left/right/top/bottom or as a tab; drag a docked title or a tab out to float it; the bars resize; closed windows keep their place. Keyboard: Ctrl+Alt+arrows dock the focused window at an edge, Ctrl+Alt+Enter tabs it with the spectrum, Ctrl+Alt+U undocks, Ctrl+Alt+PageUp/PageDown switch tabs; the same in View > Docking > Window. The layout is kept per mode in the mode's layout ini ([OnAirDock][Layout], so Reset layout clears it); named layouts per mode in dataDir()/dock_layouts.txt (Save / Load / Delete, Reset docked layout, Undock all). Pure tree in app/ui/dock_tree.h (test dock_tree). UI in app/ui/ui2_dock.cpp. Dev flags: --dock, --docktree <text>, --dockselftest (scripted ImGui input in a hidden run: drag to a side target, drag a docked title out, drop as a tab, the four shortcuts, a splitter, named layouts, ini round trip, docking off; 11/11 pass). Bug found by it: a docked window could not be dragged out (ImGui moves the window before the hook, so the press is now tested against the docked title bar). Hidden --shot (renamed copy, own DECT2_DATA_DIR): Spectrum+Map tabs, Modes, List, Log, Analysis, no ImGui errors. Unrelated: the non-docked Sidebar layout logs an ImGui "SetCursorPos extends boundaries" error in ##main2 (already there). Not done: tab reordering by drag, keyboard resizing of the bars, docking in the classic interface.

## 2026-10-23 dec-data-030: ADS-B 2 to 2.5 dB more sensitive, ISM decoders tested, LoRa thresholds measured
ADS-B demodulator (core/src/adsb/adsb_demod.cpp): a looser preamble test (the four pulses together against the quiet windows, then a contrast check of the first 56 bits; a peak must be the highest within 1.5 us), a coherent slicer for frames that fail (carrier offset from lag-1 products refined by an FFT over the pulse phases, complex half-bit integrals along a running phase reference), and sequence estimation (3 taps by least squares, 2-state Viterbi, twice) against the radio filter's smearing. 90 % points (test_adsb_rx sweep, 2000 frames, +-200 kHz offsets, random phase): 4 Msps 12.0 -> 9.8 dB, 10 Msps 12.6 -> 9.9, 2.4 Msps 12.3 -> 10.9, 2 Msps 17.8 -> 15.3; 0 phantoms in every cell, noise-only candidates 0 per second. Touched frames: impulses 72 -> 92 of 111, Mode A/C 73 -> 85 of 90, overlapped 35 -> 50 %. Head-to-head on generated 2.4 Msps cu8 airspaces (60 aircraft, 30 s; messages): weak 878 vs readsb 705 / dump1090-fa --fix 679; medium 2992 vs 1925 / 1850; strong 10237 vs 7587 / 6965 (OnAir before: 724 / 2207 / 8579). "Failed the CRC" counts only candidates that pass the older test. Cost: up to about 1.7x CPU in a crowded weak airspace (still over 10x real time at 2.4 Msps). Knobs for comparisons: AdsbReceiver::setDetector / setCoherent, adsbtool rx --older-demod, test_adsb_rx sweep / ber / stress. docs/modes/adsb.md (not in git) still has the old table.
AIS (core/src/ais/ais_phy.cpp): a coherent detection pass after the discriminator and its sequence search (half-sine windows on the bit boundaries, fine frequency by FFT, phase reference over 32 boundaries, four-state Viterbi over the running phase); it reports the frequency it found, which the satellite mode's cancellation needs. test_ais_sweep 90 % point 2.6 -> 1.5 dB in 48 kHz (9.6 -> 8.5 dB Eb/N0); limit tightened to 2.5 dB.
ISM: the 16 rtl_433 decoders of ism-315-030 build and pass (the FT-004B test vector was one bit too long; ERT IDM got a test vector).
LoRa raw: test_loraraw_rx sweep (30 frames per point, 125 kHz, 16 bytes, CR 4/5): at Semtech's limit L (SF7 -7.5 ... SF12 -20 dB) 80 / 83 / 97 / 97 / 97 / 100 % for SF7 - SF12, 100 % at L+2; Auto SF costs nothing. No change needed.
Laptop worker (Linux, run 39944186648): adsb / ais / ism / loraraw / lorawan / uat tests 40 of 40 built ones passed (fuzz_uat_proto and mesh_lorawan were not in the target list). The base integ-30 does not build there without -Wno-changes-meaning (core/src/fm/fm_rx.cpp:364, struct Biquad, a GCC error; not touched here). On the loaded Mac, ais_engine and adsb_engine lock-time checks fail with and without these changes.
Not done: AIS-catcher head-to-head (built from GitHub, but it decodes 0 messages of OnAir's generated files at any SNR, rate, format or model; cause not found), MLAT, history map, MQTT. Untested on real signals.

## 2026-10-23 DVB-S2 decoder near the threshold (dec-satTv-030)
LDPC: layered min-sum with the two-minimum box-plus correction (LdpcCode::decodeFast/decodeFast8 boxCorr; Metal + D3D11 kernels via a negative alpha; Vulkan stays plain, CPU retry covers it) and s2LdpcAlpha(mod, rate, short) measured per MODCOD incl. all 55 S2X (the per-rate table cost 0.1-0.5 dB; QPSK/8PSK 3/5 and S2X 8PSK 23/36 failed every frame 0.15 dB above the table). 8-bit decoder takes alpha (1/32) and its float fallback uses it (was 0.78); 32APSK+ on float. VL-SNR on the corrected decoder (alpha 0.95/0.92). Acquisition: second header searched ±8 symbols, runner-up test relative to depth, relock after 16 failed frames with good headers (max 3). Whole-receiver thresholds (FER<=15%, vs table 13): QPSK 1/4 +0.1 -> -0.3, 1/2 +0.2 -> -0.1, 3/5 +0.5 -> <=0, 8PSK 3/5 +0.3 -> <=0, 32APSK 4/5 +0.2 -> <=0, short QPSK 3/5 +0.3 -> <=0. Lock (first packet) 0.35-0.6 s at QEF+3 dB, 1-2 MBd. Synthetic signals only; low symbol rates left to dvbs-narrow-030; dvbs_rx.cpp untouched.

OpenGL ES not added (needs a different loader, shaders and ImGui GLSL ES setup). Nothing else in app/gfx/ needs more than GL 3.0 (only VAO, FBO, texelFetch, R8/RG8). ## 2026-10-23 Spectrum labels line up (fix-labels-230-030, #230)
Root cause: the waterfall reserves room for its Waterfall button and autolabel_logic.h used that narrowed pixel range as the whole axis, so waterfall labels sat on a stretched scale; boxes were also clamped to the plot edge, so they stuck while panning. New: View.ax0/ax1 = whole plot rectangle for the frequency-to-pixel map (xOf, same in both panes), px0/px1 only limit where boxes go. A name with its frequency in view is centred on it and clipped by the plot; a name with a nominal band (Label.bwMhz, from the band plan) stays while the band overlaps the view, box moved inside, stub arrow at the edge, no pointer. Yellow left stripe removed; a small triangle under the name points at the exact frequency; a thin bar on its own lane (2 lanes, no overlaps) shows the nominal band. Tests in tests/app/test_autolabel.cpp. Not verified on the reporters Mac.
## 2026-10-23 Sensitivity / lock-time harness (sens-harness-030)
tools/sens/sens_sweep (+ run.sh, compare.py, docs/SENSITIVITY.md, workflow sens-quick.yml): per mode, per scenario (AWGN, 2/20 ppm carrier, off-centre, 50 ppm clock, echo, TU6, Rician, phase noise, combined), seeded trials at 1 dB steps: success rate, time to lock (median/p90), threshold SNR (>=90 %), PER. Reuses the test_iq_modes generators and receiver set-up; 26 modes registered, gap list in the doc (`sens_sweep --gaps`). Quick local run of dvbt, dab, fm, fm-rds worked (DVB-T AWGN 18 dB, DAB 3 dB, FM audio 10 dB, RDS 18 dB). Full baseline built on GitHub by pushing sens-baseline-run-030 (a workflow is only dispatchable from the default branch); the narrowband blocks (pager ... p25) were not run locally and report "no valid output" if their set-up is wrong.

## 2026-10-23 DVB-T / T2 / C optimisation, second pass (perf2-dvbt-030)
Profiled with bench_dvb (stage CPU) and `sample`, every change compared old against new on the same input. Exact (bit for bit): Viterbi NEON step with two branch metrics instead of four and renormalisation every 16 steps (64.6 instructions per step), table depuncturing (3.8x), split-array FFT input, gathered equaliser, GridInterpolator keeps its delay phasors and convCorrShort runs 32 outputs at a time (-50%), T2 LDPC in blocks of 20 checks with the min-sum state in registers (-7% per iteration), T2 demapper by a minimum tree in NEON (-45%), cell de-interleaver without divisions, four phasor chains side by side, PLP cells straight into the stream, NEON ClockCorrector (1.9x), byte-table CRC-8 in the TS unpacker (T2 TS out -65%), buffers kept with their threads. Not bit-identical inside, TS digests unchanged: DVB-T one ExactResampler for rate change plus clock correction (36 taps instead of 32 + 32), T2 resampler kernel sized for the occupied band (32 instead of 64 taps). Dropped: DVB-C four partial sums (digest changed), int16 LDPC prototype (same FER, not faster). Result under the guard: DVB-T 8 MHz -40% (0.675 -> 0.404 CPU s/s), T2 32K 256-QAM -21%, DVB-C unchanged; whole engine (dect2cli, 8 MHz DVB-T at 10 Msps) 79% -> 55% of a core. Figures and limits in docs/PERFORMANCE-DVB.md. The laptop runner cannot build this ref yet: core/src/fm/fm_rx.cpp:364 fails with GCC 13 -Wchanges-meaning (from integ-30).
## 2026-10-23 HF/amateur decoders (dec-hf-030)
FT8/FT4/FT2: a second ("deep") stage after the unchanged plain passes: WSJT-X's nsym 2/3 coherent soft bits (FT4/FT2 2/4, with the per-symbol phase step removed), order-1/2 OSD on strong candidates, AP type 1 (CQ bits known); OSD/AP decodes must be plain QSO types with no unknown hash. Synthetic, 8 stations/slot, SNR in 2500 Hz, 50% point: FT8 -19.8 -> -21.0 dB (WSJT-X guide -21), FT4 -15.0 -> -16.3 (-17.5), FT2 -11.8 -> -13.0; crowded FT8 slot (100 overlapping stations, -20..0 dB) 83 -> 97 decodes, old decode sets kept (superset checked), 0 false on 60 FT8 / 100 FT4 / 100 FT2 noise slots; CPU per crowded slot about 4.6 -> 6.6 s. Head-to-head on the same WAVs, ft8_lib (MIT, built in scratch): 50% at about -18.8 dB, crowded slot 39/100. WSJT-X jt9 not built (no fftw3f on the Mac). JT65: random erasures drawn by reliability rank (3000 trials), -24.0 -> -24.4 dB. WSPR (about -30) and JT9 (-26) already at the bar; a deeper Fano gave nothing measurable. PSK31/63: threshold 8 -> 5 dB, 1.5 s replay into new channels, BPSK ISI cancellation, level squelch at signal end, SNR shown held: 10% CER about 10 -> 8 dB in 31 Hz. RTTY 45/170: persistent weak lock at 16 dB, plain-difference decision with ATC only past 3:1 imbalance: 5% CER at about -3.5 -> -6 dB in 3 kHz, noise never locks. DXpedition RR73; messages now show the fox's call. bench_hf: sens / noise subcommands, BENCH_WAV export; test hfdig_ftxsens. Untested on real signals and against WSJT-X/JTDX/fldigi binaries; selective fading for the RTTY change not simulated.

## 2026-10-23 OnAir Web decoders (webonair-decoders-030)
The receivers as shared web decoders (server/web/web_decoders.{h,cpp}, built with ONAIR_WEB_SESSION): ADS-B (aircraft + positions), AIS, APRS/packet, FT8/FT4/WSPR (decodes), SSTV, WEFAX and Meteor-M LRPT (pictures as PNG strips, rows sent once, nothing stored on the host), RDS, CW, RTTY.
